2a1(1): @##////sangs rgyas stong dang rtsa cig gis dang dregs pa phyag na rdo rje nyid la mthu' stobs kyi bdag por dbang bskur nas//gnyen po stobs kyi 
2a2(1): lhar byin gyis brlabs nas/de nas phyag na rdo rje nyid kyis kyang mthun 'jug thabs kyi spyod pas btul te/sgrib pa sbyangs nas bsnams nas phyung ngo //de'i tshe 'khor gyi dregs pa can 
2a3(1): rnams kyang skag cig gis mdun du lhags te 'di skad ces bskul lo//e ma ho/rtum 'khro rngam pa'i sku//'jigs gzugs 'bar ba'i sku ni rab tu 'khros//bdag cag kun 
2a4(1): rgyal srog bral rab tu nye//lnga 'dus thig le 'od kyi gong bu nas//rtsa sbubs las kyis 'brel ba'i srog snying 'bul//thugs rje chen pos dbang bskur byin brlabs mdzod//ces 
2a5(1): dregs pa can thams cad kyis rang rang gi srog gi snying po mngos sol du phul ste bstan pa'i bsrungs mar khas blangs so//de'i byin rlabs kyis 'og min chos 'phrul gyi zhing du/dpal 
2a6(1): ya man ta ka'i dkyil 'khor longs spyod rdzogs pa'i sku'i zhal mthong ngo //de'i tshe dpal chen po nyid kyis dang drag po sgrol ba'i dbang bskur nas/lag tu can dan gyi beng bskur 
2a7(1): nas mid mgon po sgrol gying nag po bya bar btags so//dkyil 'khor chen po'i bsrungs mar bka' bsgo'o//dpal mgon po nag po thugs kyi 'khrul 'khor rtsa ba'i rgyud 
2a8(1): las gleng bzhi'i le'u ste dang po'o////de nas dpal rdo rje rtum po nyid la/rgyal po rnam thos sras kyis 'di skad ces gsol to//e ma rdo rje gtum po che//sgrol gying 
2b1(1): bdud 'dul gying chen gyi//phyi rabs yongs la skyab pa'i phyir//gnyen bsgrub las rims don bzhir rdzogs//lha spyod sa lam ci ltar lags//zhes zhus pas/bcom ldan 'das kyis 
2b2(1): bka' stsal pa//nyon cig 'dus pa'i 'khor rnams kun//log pa drag po khyad par can//btul te bsgrib sbyangs dbang bskur nas//longs spyod rdzogs sku'i zhal yang mthong //dkyil
2b3(1): 'khor chen po'i bka' bsrungs te//bsgrub pa'i rim pa 'di lta ste//ri gsum rtse 'dus dmar la gzar//dben la byin che gcan zan 'grim//bdag dbang bar chad med pa'i gnas//shing 
2b4(1): drung dur khrod la sogs par//bsod nams tshogs ni 'bad de bsag/mi bsad rjes dang yul gyi sa//ro bsregs pa la srin rdor sbyar//ma d+hal mda' gang dug khrag gdab//gru gsum mthing 
2b5(1): nag zla gam dmar//mu khyud gru chad khyams dang ldan//sgo bzhir rdo rje me ri bri//dbus su ga'u kha sbyar du//bsnyen pa rgyun gyi gsang sngags bri//rta lug khyi nag bya rog mon//bdud rtsi lan snying 
2b6(1): dar nag btum//ga bur bcug la rgya 'is btab//lho sgor thod pa mtshan ldan du//bshed po'i gtor ma bsham par bya//shar sgor 'bru sna sha khrag tshogs//nub sgor dpal bshos zur 
2b7(1): gsum nag/byang sgor mchod pa'i bye brag dgram//keng rus gsur gyi dud pa btang //dar nag ru mtshon 'phyad nas ni//zhing gi bla res ma d+hal dbul//gdug pa bka' sgo log 'dren  
2b8(1): skrad ࿒ rdo rje gur stengs zla ba'i bdan//dkon mchog bla ma yi dam lha//byang chub sems dpa' la sogs pa//mdun du dpang po bdun gyis bshags//lho phyogs dag tu kha ltas la//
3a1(1): @#//dpal gyi 'phrin las rdzogs par bya//de rjes rnam gsum cho ga yis//rdo rje rtum por rang nyid gsal//e badzra pa ne a ti pa ta ye hUM phaT//
3a2(1): stong rdzogs mgon po bskyed par bya//smin mtshams 'bra lngas dbang chen bskyed//de stengs 'brum las pho brang bsam//bdud dang srin po nyi bzla'i steng //dun las sngags brjod mgon 
3a3(1): po nyid//thing nag dbu gsum phyag drug pa//g.yas ser gnon ljang mig gsum 'phro//dbu skra kham nag thod sbrul gtams//sku la dar nag ber chen gsol//
3a4(1): bang chen rdo rje phur pa g.yas//thod khrag zhags pa dbyig rdo g.yon//g.yon na nag mo 'jigs byed ma//mig gsum skra rjes 'jigs par bsgrad//g.yas khyud 
3a5(1): g.yon pa dung dmar stob//bdud kyi mgon po ri nag spungs//bdud nag dar nag thod pas brgyan//khram shing bdud zhags rta nag zhon//bdud kyi sbrul bar bcas pa bskyed//
3a6(1): shar du srog bdag kar la 'tsher//dar kar na gza' 'khor lo gri//stag ral zig shubs rta kar bcibs//rgyal phran 'bum dang bcas pa bskyed//byang shar bdud mo mthing nag rngam//
3a7(1): zhing stag thod sbrul khrag ral rtibs//dar nag ru mtshon thod khrag 'phen//ma bdud dbus na dre nag zhon//lho stod klu gnyan ljang nag rngam//chu dar nam gza' sbrul gyi thod//sbrul zhabs 
3a8(1): sbal pa'i rgyal mtshan phyar//gdon gsum dbus na chu rta bcibs//byang stod btsan dmar bse rta bcibs//bse khrab dar dmar stag gzib phub//dar dmar ru mtshon btsan 
3b1(1): zhags 'dzin//kha bzhi btsan dang bcas pa bskyed//lho shar gying chen ljang nag rngam//zab slag stag thod rdo rje snying //the brang dbus na seng ge bcibs//gru chad mon pa mon mo'i tshogs 
3b2(1): de ltar gsal bar gyur pa dang //spyan drang bzhugs phyag mchod pa dbul//A+oM badzra yag sha ka la g+ha na g+hu ti hri da ya//badzra gying kar rag sha ka li ka la ra tsa tri ya dza//ma ma mu khraM khrag la 
3b3(1): mu shaM hri dza dza//bdun gsum nyi shu rtsa cig tu//yab yum bod pa'i rim pa bya//de nas 'khor rnams las la bkol//rtags byung dkyil 'khor dngos grub blang //rtor ma lho'i phyogs su 
3b4(1): btang de nas drag po'i las la 'jug ces gsungs so//dpal mgon po nag po thugs kyi 'khrul 'khor rtsa ba'i rgyud las/snyen pa'i le'u ste gnyis pa'o////
3b5(1): de nas drag po bsgrub pa 'di gsungs so//thog mar dur khrod chen po'i nags khrod dam//gcan zan 'du zhi lha srin 'khrug pa'i gnas//rab tu 'jigs pa'i dur khrod dben pa ru//rmod 
3b6(1): pa dug mda' lta ba'i mtshon cha'i//rmod pa las kyi 'khor lo 'di dag bsgrub//rdo rje lcam dral gzungs gdon bsod nams bsag/mi shi sa la gru gsum rtseg mar bya//rtsang dang thod pa'i 
3b7(1): phreng bas rnam pa rtams//dbus su mon nam rab rabs chad thod pa ru//rgyu rkyen drug la byas pa'i ling ga bzhug/dri bo'i thod par dug dang khrag sbyar ste//drag po mthun gyi sbyor ba rdzogs par 
3b8(1): bya rgya shog gnyis la 'khor lo bca'//gang mos las mkhan lto ba ru//rang rang rdzas kyis bri zhing las gnyer brtad//'brub khung steng nas 'khor los bcad//las kyi gsang 
4a1(1): @#//sngags mthun brab zhag dgur rnag/phyi rol bsgrub dang rten gyi rdzas rnams dgram//rta nag lug nag khyi nag bya rog snying //mon dang nal 
4a2(1): bu'i snying rnams rta ru bzung //rtor ma dpal gshos nag po dar nag mda'//rtso bo nyid kyi dam rdzas dbus su bzhag/dri mda' bshos bu nag po bdud la mchod//mda' dang dar kar 
4a3(1): bshos bu srog bdag la//rme sha 'bru drug gtor ma ma mo la//kar gtor klu sman sna tshogs na ga la//dri mda' sha khrag bshos dmar btsan la mchod//dar mda' za 'og rdo rje legs 
4a4(1): pa la/dam rdzas rnams kyi rten ni rab tu bca'//stod pa'i ngang las bdag nyid rdo rjer 'khros//las mkhan sgrol gying yab yum gsal bas bskyed//sprul pa drug po dod nas grub pa ste//
4a5(1): spyan drang bzhugs phyag mchod pa dbul//srog gi snying po 'gugs byed lcags kyu yin//dril bsdebs rkyang pa rnal 'byor nyams dang sbyar//de nas srog gi 'khor lo bskor bar bya//ces 
4a6(1): gsungs so//dpal mgon po nag po thugs kyi 'khrul 'khor rtsa ba'i rgyud las//drag po rdzas dang bca' gzhi'i le'u ste gsum pa'o////de nas yang dag 
4a7(1): bshad bya ba//bdun dril 'khor lo bskor byed na//dpal bo gang yin rtags sam bshed//las mkhan nyid la mtshon bskur ste//'khrul 'khor rnam pa drug tu bskor//rnal dang 
4a8(1): bzlog dang lto dkrug dang //bsgrub dang spogs dang shi sa yin//mgon po gsang ba'i yang snying de//las rnams kun gyi mtha' ru sbyar//dbye dgug las kyi stengs su 
4b1(1): bshad 'khor lo nyi ma dgu ru bskor//bsad pa las kyi mtshon cha 'di//bdud mda' gsum dang khrag la stim//'khor lo ling ga dag la bsnun//skabs su thod pa'i mda' 
4b2(1): yis brdeg/ma grub dam tshig nyams pas bshags//drag sngags gsang ba'i 'khor los bzlog/spogs pa'i drag sngags gdug pa'i mtshon//'khor lo g.yas su bskor bar bya//
4b3(1): steng 'og 'khor lo'i zhal yang bzlog/drag sngags gdug pa'i dpal yang zlog/ma grub spogs pa'i las 'di yang //las byed cig la mtshon cha skur//yang na dregs pa'i las mkhan 
4b4(1): nyid rdo rje legs pa nyid la bcol//srog 'khor sngags kyi grong khyer dkrug/lha dang las byed lto dkrugs pas//las la mi 'gro'i dbang med de//khrag skyug 'o dod 'bod par 
4b5(1): 'gyur lto dkrug gnad kyi drag sngags 'di//blo la mi gnas tug phrad bdeg/blo dman rmongs pas bya ba min//sngags kyi 'od zer dbugs la zhon//rtsa rgyud shes pa brlag par bsam 
4b6(1): sngags kyi 'khor lo bskor zin nas//gzugs dang mthun pa'i gnas su mnan//las rdzogs the tshom med zhes bshad//ces gsungs so//dpal mgon po nag po thugs kyi 
4b7(1): 'khrul 'khor rtsa ba'i rgyud las//drag sngags 'khor lo bskor ba'i le'u ste bzhi pa'o////de nas yang dag bshad bya ba//de'i 
4b8(1): drag sngags 'di lta ste//e badzra hi la rdubs ni lam b+ha sa a ne a ne hri ta ye//badzra yag sha ka la ka ma ha g+ha na g+ha ti hri ta ya//badzra gying ka ra rag sha ka li//ma ma mu khram khrag la mu sham 
6a1(1): le'u drug tu bshad//'od nag khyil dang mon nag dang //dmag 'dren zhib can mun pa thibs//g.yag rog ban btsun bdud kyi rtags//skyes pa skya shar seng ge dang //rta zhon 
6a2(1): 'od dkar rgyal po'i rtags//bud med nag mo skyas khyu spra//ma sring chung ma ma bdud rtags//rta dmar zhon dang skyes pa ni//dar mdung dmar po thogs pa dang //khra dang 'ug pa btsan gyi rtags//mtsho   
6a3(1): dang sbal sbrul sdig pa dang //gdug pa'i cho 'phrul klu'i rtags//khrag dang spyang gu la sogs te//zhang blon bzang po dge snyen no//rang rang mi rtar bcas pa dang //dngos rab nyams su byung na 'bring //dreg+sa  
6a4(1): pa gang nye mtshon cha bskur//bsad cing bshad byed dril la bya//lce ston sngags chad bka' rgyud rtem//longs spyod 'gyed na tshogs kyis bskang //sngags dang thibs dang btsun pas ded//rnon zhing 
6a5(1): khyi nag dom nag rmug/g.yag rog brdung na ldog pa'i rtags//mdos dang gtor ma tshogs kyis bskang //des kyang ma phan shi sa bstan //ces gsungs so//dpal mgon po nag po thugs kyi 
6a6(1): 'khrul 'khor rtsa ba'i rgyud las rmi rtags kyi le'u ste drug pa'o////de nas yang dag bshad bya ba//hU~M dur khrod chen po lcang lo can//ma la ya'i gnas mchog nas//
6a7(1): gnas lcags dbur mo rab tu ta la la//rag ta'i rgya mtsho'i rba rlong me re re//zhing chung kyeng rus lhu dum khrol lo lo//'dab chags sha za sna tshogs nyi li li//gsur chen dud pa'i tshogs rnams   
6a8(1): langs se langs//zhing chen g.yang gzhi'i tshogs rnams lhubs se lhubs//de 'dra'i gnas mchog rab tu 'jigs pa nas//gying chen yab yum 'khor bcas sku skyoMd cig/hU~M bdud   
6b1(1): 'dul sgrol gying chen po mi bzad pa//thod 'khar po brang 'bar ba'i dbus phyogs nas//bdud dang srin po byi chings snol ba'i bdan//gsal byed nyi zla pad+ma 'od du 'bar//bsreg   
6b2(1): byed me dpung 'bar ba'i rlong dkyil nas//bdud 'dul dbang phyug sgrol gying chen po ni//sku mdog nag po dug chen skyil pa 'dra//dbu gsum phyag drug zhabs bzhi skyang skum dgrad//g.ya(sa)
6b3(1): ser g.yon ljang nag po 'jigs pa'i dbus//spyan gsum sdang mig skal pa'i me ltar 'bar//dam nyams lus srog dbang po bag chags bsreg/zhal sdangs mche rtsigs ljags 'gril tshems
6b4(1): dang dgrad//khams gsum zhal du rngub cing dgra dgyegs srog rtsa gcod//zhal nas rngam pa'i nga ro 'brug stong ldir//khams gsum dgra dgyegs ma lus zil gyis gnon//smang ra smin 
6b5(1): ma skal pa'i glog ltar khyug/bdud dang srin po dgra dgyegs rdul du rlog/shangs nas rims dang rlung nag phyogs bcur 'phro//dgra dgyegs ma lus rgyal zhing brlag par byed//ral pa khams nag 
6b6(1): srid pa'i rtse mor 'bar//thod skam 'bum gyis dbu brgyan thod du bcings//sku la dar nag 'bar ba'i bde chen la//dar lce 'bum dang me tog bzha' yis brgyan (ra)stag gi lpags pa rlon pa'i gong 
6b7(1): lag la//zhing giIg.yang gzhi mang po sbrul snyan cha ('a)gur chu lha (gang dbenas chibs ma tshang )tshigs la//sbrul nag 'bar ba'i rgyan gyis brgyan//thod pa rlon pa'i phreng ba yis//do shal se rel brgyan du byas//nyi ma zla   
6b8(1): ba gza' brgyad kyis//sku'i phyogs 'tshams brgyan du byas//zhal du mi ro gsol zhing 'phrul//g.yas kyi dang po be con bsnams//dam nyams dgra bo rdul du rlog/'bar ba

8b4(1): sa chu shing //klu sman sbyar la khru gang gzugs//kar gtor klu sman sna tshogs rten//klu'i snying khar phu dang sngags//gdug pa nyid la gzugs kyis mnan/dri dgod dag tu gsod 'dod na//
8b5(1): gong gsum sa dmar khrag tu sbyar//khru gang gzugs bya gri khrag dang //mda' dmar bshos dmar sha khrag rten//btsan gyi snying khar dza dang sngags//btsan lam rgyu sar gzugs kyis mnan//

9a5(1): srog bdag rgyal po'i 'khor lo yin//nag mo ban dmar mdung can lto//rtsa ba rtsibs dgu spu gri so//sme khrag dug sna tshogs kyis byug/khram kha nag mo'i tsa kra yin//ljang nag sbrul zhags pa 
9a6(1): 'dzin ltor//lcags mkhar bcu dang spu gri so//sbal sbrul ba dmar mdze khrag byug/klu gnyan nag po'i 'khor lo yin//dmar nag rgyu zhags mdung 'dzin lto//lcags mkhar brgyad dang 
9a7(1): grigs nas//dngar gsum dag gi rag tas byug/rno myur btsan gyi 'khor lo yin//ljang ser rdo rje snying 'dzin ltor//lcags mkharabargyad dang rtsibs brgyad la//rang bzhin dug dang dri khrag byug/

9b2(1): chu ri zlum pos bskor//gzir ba rus sbal lto ba ru//tha ma me'i 'phreng bas gtams//dregs pa'i 'khor lo gsal bar bshad//ces gsungs so//dpal mgon po nag po thugs kyi 'khrul

9b4(1): bskul//hUM che btsan dpe med mgon po ni//sku la dar nag 'jol ber gsol//dbu la sbrul gyi ral pa can//mi'i thod pas dbu la brgyan//sdang mig dmar la mche ba brtsigs//stag gi 
9b5(1): smad ra me ltar 'bar//snyan cha 'gur chu mi thod brgyan//gser ska rin chen chen sku'i brgyan//zhabs la sag ti'i lham po che//chibs su dpa' bo stag la chibs//phyag na btsan dan beng chen 
9b6(1): bsnams//g.yon na thod khrag zhal du gsol//g.yon na dpal ldan re ma ti//sku mdog dmar nag ral pa can//g.yas pa bdud kyi khram bam bsnams//g.yon na nad kyi skyal pa 'dzin//
9b7(1): chibs su dred rta rkang gsum bcibs//dra ba dgu'i ste len can//khor ni mon pa 'bum gyis bskor//khyed kyi thugs dam dus la bab//gzhan yang dregs pa skul ba ni//hUM bdud yul kha rag 
9b8(1): sgo dgu nas//bdud rgyal ri nag spungs pa ni//sku mdog mthing nag 'jigs pa'i gdong //ral pa nag po sprin ltar 'thibs//sku la dar nag ber chen gsol//thod pa rlon pa'i 'phreng bas brgyan//
10a1(1): @#//dbu la dar thong nag po bcings//g.yas na bdud kyi khram shing bsnams//g.yon na bdud zhags nag po bsnams//chibs su bdud rta bra nag bcibs//
10a2(1): 'khor ni ya bdud bsam mi khyab//bdud khar nang nas 'dir spyon la//dgra la thibs nad drag po thong //hU~M rgyal yul nam mkha' phyad phyod nas//srog bdag 'od lha dkar po ni//sku mdog kar 
10a3(1): gsal nyi zla'i 'od//sku la dar kar ral ka gsol//dbu la dar kar 'gying thod bcings//stag ral g.yas la gzig shubs g.yon//g.yas pa gser gyi 'khor lo 'phen//g.yon 
10a4(1): pa shel gyi za gri bsnams//chibs su rta kar phud dmar chibs//'khor ni rgyal 'phran bsam mi khyab//shel mkhar nang nas 'dir spyon la //dgra la smyo 'bog rgyal nad thong //hU~M ma 
10a5(1): yul byang shar mtsho rting nas//ma bdud rdo rje sder chen ni//sku mdog nag po ha la'i gdor//khrag ral dmar nag bu yug 'bebs//kha rlangs sngon dmar nad sprin chags//sku stod zhing   
10a6(1): chen g.yang gzhi la//sku smad stag dang re ldes brgyan//g.yas pa dar nag ma bdud g.yob//g.yon pa thod khrag rims dal 'babs//chibs su dre nag rting kar chibs//'khor na 
10a7(1): bdud mo bsam mi khyab//re nag gur nas 'dir spyon la//dgra la dal thong la sgrol//hU~M klu yul nar sing sngon nas//klu gnyan nag po srog gcod ni//sku mdog dmar nag 'jigs 
10a8(1): pa'i zhal//lcer bu'i lus la 'jigs pa'i gzugs//sku la chu srin g.yang gzhi gsol//rigs lnga sbrul gyi thod gdengs can//dbu la chu dar ljang ku bcings//g.yas na sbrul    
10b1(1): zhags bla re 'gugs//g.yon pa gnyan sbal cho 'phrul stong //chibs su klu rta ljang nag chibs//'khor na gdug pa bsam mi khyab//rin chen mkhar nas 'dir spyon la//dgra la mdze shu 
10b2(1): po la 'bras thod//hUM btsan yul zangs thang dmar po nas//btsan rje shu 'o lag rangs ni//sku mdog dmar nag rngam pa'i zhal//sku la bse'i khrab dar dmar gsol//dbu la le brgan dmar 
10b3(1): po bcings//stag ral g.yas la gzig shubs g.yon//bse 'phub dmar nag me 'od 'khyil//g.yas na dar dmar ru mtshon 'phyar//g.yon na btsan zhags dmar po 'phen//chibs su 
10b4(1): btsan rta dmar po chibs//'khor ni kha bzhi btsan gyis bskor//bse mkhar nang nas 'dir spyon la//dgral dri thabs cho dgu thong //hUM shar phyogs rin gling thang nas//gying chen rdo rje legs 
10b5(1): pa ni sku la za 'og ral ka gsol//dbu la stag thod khra bo bcings//stag ral g.yasal gzig shubs g.yon //g.yas na gnam lcags rdo rje phyar//g.yon na snying chen zhal du gsol//
10b6(1): chibs su seng ge dkar mo chibs//rol du lcags spyang ston mo khrid//slad nas zangs kyi phar brgyug/sprul pa lcags kyi khra rgod 'gyed//'khor ni the brang 'bum gyis bskor//lung thang khar nas 
10b7(1): 'dir spyon la//dgra la pho log hur thums thong //hUM srid pa'i dregs pa chen po rnams//phyag na rdo rje'i bka' byung gis//rang rang las kyi skal longs la//bcol ba'i las rnams grub par 
10b8(1): mdzod ces gsungs so//dpal mgon po nag po thugs kyi 'khrul 'khor rtsa ba'i rgyud las//dregs pa can bsgrub pa'i le'u ste bcu gcig pa'o////
11a1(1): /de nas yang dag bshad bya ba//drag po thun gyi bsgrub pa ni //'bub tu thong ngan ling ka bca' //steng nas dregs pa'i tsa kra brgyang //nub tu thod ngan  
11a2(1): lcags phur la//sor brgyad dug ngar bdud du bskyed//shar du nyungs kar rgyal por bskyed //lho ru mdze'i thod dug sna tshogs//gdug pa'i klu gnyan nag por skyed //byang shar thod ngan khrag 
11a3(1): sna tshogs//nad mtshon brlabs la ma bdud bskyed//lho shar lcags phye zangs phye gying //spyang rgyus dug thug srin can rgyud //bya khra rkang dang mda' bya rog sgro//dri lcags mde bcug     
11a4(1): btsan bskyed bya//bdag nyid mgon po nag por bsgom//bla khugs lha 'khrel thun rnams rdeg/dang po dri lcags phur pa ded//klad pa'i brgyas su thog ltar thim//mgo bkas klad 
11a5(1): pa bzhu zhing sreg/gnyis pa nyungs kar rgyal po ste//'grin par tsha tsha'i gzer ltar thim//rtsa brgyad dbugs dkag myos shing brlabs//gsum pa dug thun snying kar gdab//mtshon cha 
11a6(1): sna tshogs me lcer thim//rtsa brgyad khrag skams me 'bar bsad //bzhi pa khrag thun lte bar gdag/rten gyi rtsa bcad lus ri skyel//lnga pa phye mthun gsang bar bdeg/de nyid me mtshon thog   
11a7(1): ltar thim//srid par skye ba'i rgyud rtsa bcad//drug pa mda' mthun kar nag mtshams//mtshon cha yis rtsa bcad shes pa 'pho//dangs ma chos nyid bde ba yis//phung po lhag ma gying tshogs   
11a8(1): rol  thon nas ling ka thod pa de //dred zhabs mon pa klu la rtad//de'i gsang sngags 'di lta ste//dun ru tra ru thums b+ha sti//rag sha khram ki li ki la ya shag rbad //ra tsa pa la a shug 
11b1(1): me/byi ru na hi ta myogs thibs rbad//na ga hU sti sa nag du ha//ha la tsa ma ri tsi ta la shag rbad rni dza//ma trag rag sha nag mo ti lam b+h+yo//rag ta khrag byi ta ti ni pa tsa li stubs stam pa ya nan;
11b2(1): badzra tri nas du kha dun tri//hU sti yam ling g+hu ha la ma ra ya rbad//shu la tsa ra tri sha ma dza yaM//srog la thums ri li li dgra bo la b+h+yo b+h+yo//gnyan gnon stobs chen mgon po'i sngags//
11b3(1): ces gsungs so//dpal mgon po nag po thugs kyi 'khrul 'khor rtsa ba'i rgyud las//zor mthun gyi le'u ste bcu gnyis pa'o////
11b4(1): de nas yang dag bshad bya ba//bsod nams tshogs bsags ma d+hal rtsig/nang du thod ngan skam thun rdzas//ser bsrung phyag rdor phur pas gzir//dar nag steng du dong bu spyad//
11b5(1): mi rta khyi ra snol khrag dang //g.yag phag khrag blugs steng du bzhug/rten rdzas srog dang mthun pa'o//gza' la sha rul gtor ma bca'//dong bur mgon po yab yum gsal//bka' 
11b6(1): stod bza' rgod dud ka 'jigs//sprin gos dbu la gzha' dang klog/thod zor ser 'bebs 'brug la zhon//sprul pa drug dang thor pa thogs//rang rang kha dog 'brug la zhon//spyan dang 
11b7(1): stim mchod rim pa bya//'pho 'dus skam thun rdzas la gril//dgun zla tha chungs snyen pa bya//mtsho dkyil rka 'don rlung tshab na//bag phye khrag la bu shag bya//gza' rgod lto bar 'khor lo bri//
11b8(1): ril bu lhar bskyed dbang bskur ngar//dus bzhir dong bur thun du brab//gzi mdangs klog khyug nga ro 'brug/shangs nas rlung nag chub ma chub//kha nas ser 'bebs 'bru bcud sdud//jag 
12a1(1): @#//pa skugs su bcug par bsam//skya bshar lug khyu 'grim pa dang //'khyag pa thor na rta bsgrub bya//mi rta khyi phag thod pa bzhir//thun rdzas gtor ma ri 
12a2(1): lu bgo  gza' g+hon mon thod mu ma'i dkyil//rta thod rmu btsan gnam gzer rtse//chu dmig khyi thod ma mo klu//lha rten phag thod bdud dang rgyal//ser bsrungs rnams la phyag rdor phur//
12a3(1): gnam thod kar lam sdo lam sbreng //steng gsum dgra yul dregs pa'i gnas//gza' rgod gnas pa'i dus dag la//zor thab du bas lha snol dbab//de'i drag sngags 'di lta ste//
12a4(1): mgo la rdo rje stobs chen no//A+oM badzra yag sha ma ha ka la g+ha na g+hu ti hri da ya//ra hu la ya ma snying ya ma khrag/ma ma mu khram khrag la mu sham hrim dza dza//A+oM ma ha ka la g+ha na g+hu  ti dri da ya//
12a5(1): phroM phroM hril hril yaM yaM hril//ra hu la ya ma snying ma ma khrag/phroMm phrom hril hril yaM yaM hri la//ru ta ra tsa/ma ma shu lan ga badzra sa du//phroM phroM hril hri la yaM yaM hril//rmu ri skya ri se ti  
12a6(1): taM ram  shar shar yaM shar drag tu shar//yaM yaM hril hril shar shar//phob yaM/hril yaM shar drag tu phob//thog thog rim rim yul 'dir ser ba drag tu phob ya//de pa na g+ha shu la snying gzer spur 
12a7(1): nan dza de yis the tshom med pa bgrub//ngan thabs sbyin bsreg khrus dang pra ma lus bshad pa'o//ces gsungs so//dpal mgon po nag po thugs kyi 'khrul 'khor rtsa ba'i rgyud las//
12a8(1): thog ser dbab pa'i le'u ste bcu gsum pa'o////de nas yang dag bshad bya ba//rang la log na mchod pa dang //las bcol grub na     
12b1(1): bskang ba'i rdzas//ma d+hal steng du mi lpags bting //de stengs 'bru lnga'i ri rab la//kha dog legs par mtshon gyis byug/lo bzangs dpag bsam shing chen la//mgon po yab yum 
12b2(1): sku gzugs gdags//bshos bu dpal bshos nag po la//thid nag nam mkha' sna lnga'i 'od//nag mo zangs mig rma bya'i mdongs//dar nag ba dan phan dang bcas//lug nag bya 
12b3(1): nag khyi nag gi/gzugs bya ri rab rtse la gzugs//de ni mgon po yab yum rdzas//shar du nam kha g.yu mig dkar//dar kar ba dan phan dang bcas/ra kar spre kar khyi   
12b4(1): kar gzugs//bshos bzangs 'od lha dkar po'i rdzas//lho shar gser mig g.yu yis spras//rin chen rkang bu khra spyang ra//bshos bzangs dag bla gying gi rdzas//lho sman nam rkyang    
12b5(1): ser ljang la sbal ljong sdig pa nyi sram bya//gdug pa gsum gyi rdzas yin no //nub tu nam rgyang nag po la//dar nag phan dang sa lug g.yag/bshos bzangs nag po bdud kyi rdzas//
12b6(1): byang du nam rkyang ba dan dmar//ra rta bya dmar bshos bzangs btsan//byang shar thing nag nam   mkha' gur//sha khrag bya lug bdud mo'i rdzas//srog chags kha dog rigs su sgyur//
12b7(1): gzhan yang khram shing mda' dang 'phang //tha mar sha khrag gtor mas bkang //keng rus gsur gyi dud pa btang //steng nas dar nag gur dang 'bub//mchod pa bshams la bshad po'i  
12b8(1): gzhung   srang la las kyi gshin rje mchod//thugs dam bskang bshags drag tu bskul//zor dang bcas pa lho ru btang //de yis skongs par nga yis bshad //ces gsungs so  
13a1(1): @#//dpal mgon po nag po thugs kyi 'khrul 'khor rtsa ba'i rgyud las//bskang ba 'dos kyi le'u ste bcu bzhi pa'o////
13a2(1): de nas yang dag bshad bya ba//las dang rkyen ni drag po las//drag po'i chi bdag gshin rjer bskyed//dug lnga'i rang bzhin rnam rtog rkyen//gti mug bye ba gu rings te//zhe sdang gza' 
13a3(1): rgod ra hu la//nga rgyal sgrol gying mgon po nyid//'dod chags klu bdud ti pa tsa//phra dog sgrol byed ma ru tse//smon lam dbang gis stan pa bsrung //ri rab gling la gnas bcas nas//
13a4(1): khams gsum tshe srog srang la 'jal//mos pa spyod pa'i sa non te//tshogs lam rgyu'i mgon por bshad//sbyin tshogs stobs las yang dag byung //nga bdag med de rgyun bcad nas//
13a5(1): rigs lnga'i dkyil 'khor bsrung bar mdzad//rigs lnga sangs rgyas sprul pa yi//sa brgyad non te sgom lam gnas//lam rgyud gnas pa'i 'chi bdag go/sku lngar mi gnas ye shes

13a7(1): gis du mar snang //dam pa'i don du rang bzhin gcig/chos rnams sems su mngon gsal zhing //sems nyid stong pa'i don rtogs nas//skye shi gnyis med khrul pa'i lam//
13a8(1): mngon spyod sgral dang sdig mi mgo/dper na nam mkha' stong pa 'dra //de don rtogs na lta ba'i mchog/rtul shugs can gyi spyod pa ni//mi sha za zhing mi khrag thung //bam 
13b1(1): chen bdud rtsi lnga la rol//g.yang gzhi stag lpags ro ras 'go/dur khrod brgyan dang 'phreng ba 'grang //ngag tu sngags kyi sgra mi bcag/'di dag ldan na spyod pa'i mchog/ces gsungs so//
13b2(1): dpal mgon po nag po thugs kyi 'khrul 'khor rtsa ba'i rgyud las//lam 'bras lta spyod bstan pa'i le'u ste bco lnga pa'o////de nas 
13b3(1): rgyud gtad pa 'di gsungs so//nga'i 'khor du 'dus pa rnams//byang chub sems dpa' khro bo'i tshogs//rtsang rigs mgon po lha'i tshogs//srog len dregs pa sde brgyad tshogs//
13b4(1): bka'i gsang rgyud 'khor lo 'di//stan pa rin chen rtar gyi mchog/drag sngags me dbal spu gris bcod//rtsa ba'i man ngag 'phrul gyi bka'//thugs rjes 'gro drug rnams kyi mgon//
13b5(1): yang gas shin tu zab pa'i thugs//cig gi don shes kun la khrul//srog ces bya bar gang gsungs pa//sde brgyad srog gi de nyid ston//'khor lo shes su gang gsungs pa//drag sngags 
13b6(1): dug gi dpal can gyis//dmigs pa'i lus srog gdug byed gcod//rtsa ba zhes su gang gsungs pa//bsgrub pa kun gyi don dang ldan//rgyud ces bya bar gang gsungasapa//lta spyod 
13b7(1): dkyil 'khor lam 'bras dang //snyen bsgrub lam gyi rim pa kun//tshang zhing 'brel pas rgyud ces bya ba//'di dag bsgrub pa'i rim pa la//gzer chen drug gi man ngag gces//
13b8(1): dang po man ngag gnad kyi gzer//dril sdeb rkyang pa'i don rnams kyang //rang rang bca' thabs bshad pa bzhin//de nas gshis dang gnyen po'i lha//dus gsum bder gshegs sku'i mchog 
14a1(1): @#//zil gnon bder gshegs gshin rje gshed//longs sku zhal mthong dbang bskur bas//dbang po bshis pa'i lha zhes bya//gnyen po'i lha ni rdo rje khros//
14a2(1): dus gsum sangs rgyas stong rtsa gcig/thu stobs bdag por dbang bskur ba'i//drag po gdul bya char bcad pas//gnyen po stobs kyi lha zhes bya//don gnyis lha'i snyen pa rdzogs//drag 
14a3(1): po sngags gzer ma nor bsdebs//rang rang ca kra gong du bshad//'phro 'du las kyi rim pa ni//bdag dang las mkhan sprul pa gsal//sngags kyi 'od gzer rlung la zhon//rtsa bcad khrag 
14a4(1): bzhu dangs snyigs phye//las rnams drag tu sbyor ba'i tshe//bdag nyid mgon po nag por bsgom//mtha' bsnyad rang rang rnag pa ces//slu ba med pa'i las yin no//shag thub stan pa 'jig 
14a5(1): pa'i dus//rtsi shing nags rtsal zad pa'i dus//tshe lo lnga bcu mi thub dus//rnal 'byor pa rme khrug byed pa'i dus//lha srin 'dran byed ba'i dus//de dus gdab pa'i drag sngags 'byung //zu lums 
14a6(1): ra chod spyad 'gyur na//stobs med rang nyid brlag par 'gyur//sgrol gying mgon po nyid la dri//cang mi smra na dus min pas//rang gi snying bzhin rnags la sba//bka' gsang thob na ngan sngags bzlas//
14a7(1): de dus nus pa dpag med do//gdab ga'i gnas dang gsad pa'i sa//yang gsad yan lag 'bru rgod 'di//gang la brgya ba dang 'chor mi srid//sgrol gying mgon po nag po'i//'khor lo thugs kyi 
14a8(1): thar thug rgyud//nus pa rang dbang med par 'byung //rgyud 'di gzhung du gang bsgrub pa'i//phyi rabs rnal 'byor gang yin la//nga dang dus gsum 'bu ta rnams//thugs rjes 'brel stobs rtag tu 
14b1(1): bsrung 'khor gyi dregs pa pho mos kyang //'di dag bsgrub pa'i grogs gyis shig/ces gsungs so//de nas 'khor du 'dus pa rnams kyis//bcom ldan 'das rdo rje rtum po nyid la 'grin 
14b2(1): cig tu byas te stong pa//hUM rtum rngam drag shul mthu mnga' 'bar ba'i sku//rigs sngags gsang sngags kun gyi bdag po ste//log 'dren dgyegs kyi tshogs rnams 'dul mdzad pa'i//mthu'

14b4(1): rtad pa'i le'u ste bcu drug pa'o////dpal mgon po nag po thags kyi 'khrul 'khor srog 'khor lo rtsa ba'i rgyud ces bya ba rdzogs so////
14b5(1): bram ze khrag 'thung nag po dang //bod kyi ban de sangs rgyas kyis sgyur ba'o//sngags dang mtshon cha dang bka'i bdag po gsum gyis rgyas btab bo//spyad sngas na rgyal khams phung 
14b6(1): gis dogs nas lung dang mthun pa'i gnas su rter du sbas so////sa ma ya /rgya rgya rgya/dge'o////rgya gar skad du/shri g+ha hi ka la ha la so ri g+ha ha tan tra na ma//
14b7(1): bod skad du/dpal mgon po nag po dug gi spu gri gsang ba'i rgyud ces bya ba//bcom ldan 'das dpal phyag na rdo rje la phyag 'tshal lo//'di skad bdag gis thos pa'i dus cig na//bcom 
14b8(1): ldan 'das dpal rdo rje'i snying po rngam pa'i rgyal po nyid//dur khrod chen po lcang lo can gyi gnas//bam ri brtsegs pa'i pho brang //gnam lcags ur mo'i gzhal yas khang na bzhugs so//
15a1(1): 'khor sa la gnas pa'i byang chub sems dpa' rnams dang //khro bo rngam pa rol pa'i tshogs dang //'jigs rten dregs pa pho mo'i tshogs dang //ma mo mkha' 'gro    
15a2(1): gdug pa 'dul ba'i tshogs dang //rtsang rigs lha dang rgyal po'i tshogs//sha za rngam pa 'jigs pa'i tshogs dang //thabs cig tu bzhugs so//de'i tshe rgyal po rnam thos sras zhes bya ba rang gi stan  
15a3(1): las langs te//rkyang phyag lan stong du btsal nas gsol ba//kye ma rdo rje snying po che//gsang sngags bka'i 'khor lo la//rtse gcig bsrung ba'i sgrol gying ste//mgon po nag por mtshan gsol
15a4(1): ba'i rabs rgyud stan tshigs ci ltar lags ces zhus pas//rdo rje snying pos bka' stsal pa//legs so rigs kyi bu khyod nyon//gying chen mgon po nag po 'di//rnal 'byor yongs kyi dgra 
15a5(1): bla ste࿒ rigs 'dzin yongs kyi srog shing yin//bdud 'dul rnams kyi dbang phyug po//nga'i bka'i las mkhan yin//'di'i rabs rgyud stan tshigs dang //btan la dbab pa 'di lta ste//
15a6(1): 'das pa'i dus na sngon 'byung 'byung ba//bskal pa rdzogs ldan bya ba la//lho'i 'dzam bu g+hing 'di ru//khyim bdag ke'u shi ka yang //bya ba de la bu cig yod //de ming thar pa nag po bya//
15a7(1): kho de dad pas sems g.yos nas//yul phyogs de'i dgon pa na//dge slong thub bka' gzhon nu zhes bya ba gsang sngags don rtogs nas//yod pa kho des thos pa las//dad 'phags g.yog  
15a8(1): dang bcas pa yis//de'i drung phyin gdam ngag zhus//de nyid kyis kyang gdam ngag gnang //de bzhin nyid de ma bcos na //dngos po bzhi la spyad byas kyang //nam mkha' la ni sprin bzhin te//
15b1(1): de ni rnal 'byor dam pa'i lam//de skad bdam ngag phog pa las//kho bdag dga' dgu rangs te deng //rang gi gnas su byin pa las//don gyi gdeng ni ma rnyed par//dngos po bzhi la sgra bzhin 
15b2(1): spyad nam zhig dpon g.yog ma mthun te//rtsod 'gyur dge slong dag la dris//dge slong rang bzhin gsong po des//thar pa nag po sun byung nas//dad 'phags bden no zhes gsungs pas//

22a1(1): @#//du sngags dang mi rus bri//phung sa gsum dang chu yis byug/khrul 'khor dril rtor sbyor        ba rtsam//rdzogs nas rten sba rgug cing thab//

22a3(1): sngags ni bri zhing nyungs kar sbas//yul gyi dbus su de sbas la//byung dus rang dang nyung(sa) kar 'phang //gso ba slungs dang sri'u ste//sngags zlas ma lus rang lus bcang //rang lus

22a6(1): badzra pa ne ni lam//badzra yag sha ma ha ka la dun drag rag sha ma//badzra pa badzra yag sha ma ne badzra//badzra ne ma ha ka la ni badzra//badzra ni la dun drag rag lam badzra//badzra lam

22a8(1): badzra/badzra ni la ka ha ma ne badzra//ba ne ma sha yag badzra pa badzra//ya ra ma tsa ta rag /ya ra ma tsa ta rag/yam breng la ta tsi//ya ma pra/ril thum la srog rbad//
22b1(1): badzra pa badzra yag sha ma ne//pa badzra yag sha ma ne badzra pa//yag sha ma ne badzra pa badzra//sha ma ne badzra pa badzra yag//ma ne badzra pa badzra yag sha/ne badzra pa badzra yag sha ma//
22b2(1): badzra ne ma ha ka la ni//ne ma ha ka la ni badzra//ma ha ka la ni badzra ne//ha ka la ni badzra ne ma//ka la ni badzra ne ma ha//la ni badzra ne ma ha ka ni badzra ne ma ha ka la//badzra ni la dun   
22b3(1): trag rag lam//ni la dun trag rag lam badzra//la dun trag rag lam badzra ni//dun trag rag lam badzra ni la//trag rag lam badzra ni la dun//rag lam badzra ni la dun trag 
22b4(1): lam badzra ni la dun trag rag/badzra lam rag sha ma badzra pa//lam rag sha ma badzra pa badzra//rag sha ma badzra pa badzra lam//sha ma badzra pa badzra lam rag/ma badzra pa badzra lam 
22b5(1): rag sha/badzra pa badzra lam rag sha ma//pa badzra lam rag sha ma badzra//badzra ni ne pa yag ma badzra//badzra ma ka ni ne ha la//badzra trag lam ni//dun la rag/badzra rag

22b7(1): rag trag dun la ni badzra//badzra ni la ka ha ma ni badzra/badzra ne ma sha yag badzra pa badzra//phroM phroM thibs phroM phroM 'ur hril hril//badzra badzra sha lam pa ma rag/badzra rag dun ni lam trag la//

23a1(1): @#//rag lam badzra/pa badzra ma sha rag lam badzra pa/badzra ma sha rag lam badzrapa badzra//ma sha rag la badzra pa badzra/ma sha rag la badzra pa badzra ma//

23a8(1): badzra la;badzra ne lam ma rag/badzra ni pa la badzra/badzra lam ne rag ma/sa ma ya u u ma su ka sarba sha hUM phaT//de dag gso ba'i drag sngags so//badzra ne ni ma ha ka la//

23b2(1): ne ma la badzra ka he ne ne ma la/badzra dun trag ni lam la rag/badzra ma sha lam pa rag badzra/thibs hur yaM thor thor//ser ba sha truMn sha ya phaT//staM pa ya byig nan kha thaM b+h+yo//de dag ser 
23b3(1): ba bsrung ba'o//badzra yag sha dun rag/badzra ka ha ma sha/badzra dun trag sha yag/badzra ma sha ka ha/ru pa pri sarba g+hu yaM ka pa li rgyal ha la ha la du ling has phob ya/de dag lhas dan  
23b4(1): dbab pa'o//badzra pa badzra ma la ni badzra/ma ne rag lam badzra/la ni badzra badzra/rag lam ma ne badzra/ad ma ta phya sangs sa la sham shaM/tsi ta hal yaM ti pa ra phaT//de dag bsnyon pa'i
23b5(1): drag sngags so////yag sha badzra tag ha ma sha badzra dun ka sha/ha badzra ma yag tag/ka badzra sha sha dun //byi de sha yi pra tsa li pra shag shag pra pe sha   
23b6(1): ya phaT/de dag skrad cing dbye ba'o//badzra pa badzra/badzra ne ma/badzra ni la/traM traM hring hring /ru pi ru tri a yang tse skang /de dag rengs pa'i drag sngags so//badzra yag sha badzra ka

24a1(1): @#//dril ba'i las te spyi rgyug yin//'khrul 'khor gsang bdag stobs chen 'dis//bcu gsum las kyi sngags su bshad//gshin rje 'khrul 'khor

25a1(1): @#//drag sngags bdun //yang logs gza' rgod drag sngags bdun //tha mar gzir ba'i drag sngags dgod //kha sbyar zung bzhi phag ltor bca' //mi rta khyi phag thod

25a8(1): 'dus thog thog 'ur 'ur kems//phroM phroM hril hril yam yam hril//mu ri skya ri se ri tam//shar shar yam shar drag tu phob//da ya da ya hril shar phob//yam hril yam shar

27b8(1): dun|me shug a la pa tsa tsa ra //b+h+yo lam ting mo nag sha rag trag ma//ha ru du ga na sa sti hu ga na//yaM dza shaM hri ra tsa la shu//tri dun kha du sa na tri badzra//khaM dza hur thum rni rbad shag ling 
28a1(1): @#//rbad thibs khrag mugs thun ti tsa//shag breng la rtsa ril thum la sog//e a ye badzra sa ta hil ni hril rngubs a pa ni ne lam༐

28a3(1): na ha na ga ha sa hu du du sti ga/shu ya la tri ba dza ra tsa shaM/badzra kri sa kha du na na tri srog la khram ling rbad //tsa ta dun rmugs ling pa shag dun rbad //rtsal ni shag thums khrag/hur dza thibs

28a5(1): mu mu ru sti dun tra b+ha ru /a ra me la shug pa tsa//nag mo b+h+yo sha la trag mo rag ta//na na ha sa ga ru ga sti du ru //hri shu yam ra shaM la tsa dza//
28b1(1): sa tri badzra du dun na kha tri //srog la thun rmugs ling shag rbad //tsi ta thums ra li rni hur thums dza//shag rtsa la khrag thibs breng breng rbad//

29a8(1): lcags seng ldeng phur //yab yum gsal rdzogs dug khrag ngar//bzhob gnyis dug gnyis gu gul zi//nyungs kar tsha ba sres la rnag/gang gnod rgya shog gzugs bris la༐
29b1(1): snying kar 'bru re spu gri lus//rtsa bas tha skor thod chen gnan //bdag nyid rdo rje snying po che//gying chen phur pa dril zhing gdab//khri gsum rdzogs nas ling ga dril//bka' rten   
29b2(1): mgon po'i 'og tu gnan//rdzas kyi dud pas stong nas bdag/nad pa byung na dug phur gdab //sri'u bsrung na rdzas phur srung ba g.yas par gdags//rten bcud la sogs snod rnying   
29b3(1): bdug/rnod byed rmyos shing rgyal bar 'gyur //gzhan yang yul gyi sa chu shing //lo tog 'bras bu sngo sna dang //lha klu btsan dang ser bsrung gi/rdza la ling ga dbang po dod//
29b4(1): ming byang snying po snying kar gdab//thob thod chen rnams nas 'bru gsum thun//khrag gnyis 'o ma rme rdzas ni //ma 'dres blugs la dmod pa gdab//bdag nyid rdo rje bka'

30a5(1): mi za  khri gsum sngags bzlas smin drug sba//slu ba med pa'i las yin no//gzhan yang gro ga ri sho la//mngal khrag rgya skyags 'gyi bang gis//gzugs 
30a6(1): dang ming bris snying khar srog/tha ma rtsa ba gnya' gnon so//bris la tsan dan cong zis byug/bdag nyid rdo rje gying chen gyis//dmigs pa lcags kyu zhag+sa 
30a7(1): pas drangs//rang dbang med par bdag dbang bsdu//lcags kyu phyag rgyas snying nas drangs//khri gsum rdzos gdab rang 'og sba//'gi wang cong zi can dan dmar//rgya

30b1(1): man chad dbang du 'du//de dag dmod pa 'di lto ste//e badzra hri la a ti pa ta ye//badzra lam ad nya u ne//badzra gying ka ra ma ha ka la//badzra rag sha tro ta ni pa tra dun//
30b2(1): trag shig rag sha mu sha ma ma//srog la myogs//tsa ti khas rag ta thibs//hu sti yam ling ra ya rbad//ki li ki la trig nan snying gzer sbur nan dza//hur thums dza myags  
30b3(1): thums ril rbad ya//ra hu la yam snying rag sha ga te pi pi//de ba a dza yaM ti//san ga/tig nan myags sbur dza la ram//thibs 'dus 'ur 'ur phroM phroM hril//yaM shar

30b7(1): khar gzhong rtsang ma ru//lco ga srin bya'i lce dang mig/'ug pa pha wang khyi brgya'i mig/bzang drug blugs la dar kar dril//'bru rtsang lha bshos rtsi mar byug/de steng gsang   
30b8(1): lug mar skus bzhag/bdag 'dun rdzas kyi steng du bzhag/rnal 'byor bkar gsum kha zas bza'//sha chang sgog bcong myos pa spang //stong nyid ngang las rdo rje'i sku//

31a5(1): bshos 'bru kar stong du bzhag/ting 'dzin gsal bas dud pa rtang //bdag gis sngags bzlas zhag gsum rdon//de yis thams cad mngon du smra//nas la smod la bshos

32b8(1): bzhugs na rigs 'khor mchod//de yang med na gtor tshogs rtang //des ni dngos grub 'dod 'byung //rdo rje slob dpon thugs skol zhing //ma mchod mnyes par ma byas na//

34b7(1): dang la dkyil 'khor bum dbang skur bag chags rtog pa'i bsgrib pa sbyangs//de nas phyag rgya'i dbang yang bskur //gying nyid 'jigs pa'i sku ru gsal//phyag mtshan rdzogs
34b8(1): rtad phyag na 'bar //de nas mchod pa gying la 'bul//dam rdzas dbang phyug sa bdun non //de nas slob dpon gzungs ma sbyar //thugs kar thig le gsum blugs blang //

90b5(1): ngag khyal spangs pa'i yon tan smin pa yis//ting 'dzin rtse gcig mi g.yo g.yeng ba spangs//dgos 'brel tshul gyis bstan pa skyong mdzad na//byis pa 'dul mdzad khyod la phyag 'tshal

91a1(1): @#//sbrul gyi rna cha can la phyag 'tshal bstod//log par lnga ba'i phyogs ni rnam spangs te//sangs rgyas chos las grub pa'i gzugs bsnyan khyod//

91a3(1): ba bcu las mtho ris grub//las dge dang po rab tu sbyangs byas pa//gong nas gong ma'i sa la rab gnas kyang //dge ba brtags ni dman pa'i zhing 'dir brtan//gdug pa'i tshul gyis

91a6(1): pa'i lam mchog lags//de bas lha mo khyod kyi mtshan nyid ni//'di ltar rab ldan lha mo khyod kyis khyad par ste//dang por tshogs gnyis dag par rab bsags pas//dang por sa la sbyor

91a8(1): par rab brnyes nas//de bzhin sa gsum dag pa'i pha rol son//rang sems bzhi pa'i sa la cher bsgoms pas//lnga pa'i sa la bsam gtan pha rol son//rang semasas drug pa'i sa la 
91b1(1): cher bsgoms pas//lnga pa'i sa la bsam gtan pha rol son //rang sems drug pa'i sa la cher sbyangs pas//bdun pa la ni thabs kyis pha rol son //rang sem brgyad pa dag la cher sbyangs

91b5(1): lha mo nag mo gnyis su med pa ste//don dam spros bral chos 'di 'di las btsal//don dam kun rdzob gnyis su med pas bden //'khor ba myang 'das gnyis su med par zab//
91b6(1): sems can sangs rgyas gnyis su med pa'i 'phags//las dang rnam smin gnyis med gcig gis bstod //rgyu dang 'bras bu gnyis su med pas smin //yul dang yul can gnyis med

91b8(1): gzhan gyi chos ni gnyis med ngang tshul gcig/gcig dang 'dzin pa gnyis med zab mo'i mtha' //mtho ris ngan song gnyis bral dag pa'i zhing //bzang ngan gnyis med rang bzhin

92a3(1): pa'i don//stong dang med stong gnyis med zhi ba'i ngang //'gyur dang 'gyur bral spong la phyag 'tshal bstod//sgra tshig gnyis med brjod pa med pa'i ngang //lus ngag gnyis med sems

92a8(1): dpal lha mo la 'drin las rnam bzhi'i sgo nas bstod pas//sems nyid 'phrin las rnam bzhis khyad par ni//sems nyid gud na med cing sems kyang med//kun rdzob rgyu ma tsam du rang gi 
92b1(1): sems mthun par bstan pa zhi ba dang la lha mo//zhi mdzad zhi 'gyur zhi ba'i ngang tshul can //zhi ba'i 'khor gyis bskor ba'i rtso mo ni //rtags kyi sku mdog dkar po shin tu dang //kun tu
